# Re-audit of the merged tree, 2026-09-30 (Phase 8 of the 2026-09-29 audit campaign)

Run by the coordinator after every campaign lane had merged into the local master (`6056a10`, before the publish round), on a
detached worktree copy of that commit. Three passes, the audit's own method plus a regression lens:

1. **Analyzer.** A full Roslyn analyzer build (AnalysisLevel latest-all, CA and IDE rules as warnings) of the merged tree, diffed
   position-independently against the same build of the pre-campaign commit `35725396`: 274 diagnostic keys added, 94 removed, 66
   grown; 104 of the added carried a defect signal (CA2000, CA2213, CA1001, CA2025, CA1508, IDE0059, CA1849, CA1812, CA1068) and were
   triaged one by one by four agents: 10 REAL, 94 NOISE, folded into the pool as 4 findings.
2. **Readers.** 26 slice readers (every file in full, the audit's slices re-listed against the merged tree), five cross-cutting sweeps
   (threads, resources, dead code, performance, and a new lens for non-operable surface and dangling calls), and 16 regression readers
   over `git diff 35725396..6056a10` per area, reading each hunk against the disposition that claims it. 47 of 48 agents returned; the
   known-items digest agent overran its output limit and the digest was generated mechanically from the record files instead.
3. **Verification.** 446 raw findings merged to 392; skeptic and reproducer per medium-and-up or race group with a judge on disagreement,
   one verifier per low/info group. Confirmed 383, refuted 9, uncertain 0.

| severity | confirmed |
|---|---|
| critical | 0 |
| high | 2 |
| medium | 28 |
| low | 236 |
| info | 117 |

By category: correctness 82, maintainability 81, check-cannot-fail 47, dead-code 39, interesting 38, regression 25, performance 23, race 19, dangling-call 10, non-operable 9, leak 7, security 3.

By known status: NEW 334, KNOWN_FIXED 27, KNOWN_OPEN 15, KNOWN_DELIBERATE 7.

Every confirmed item is filed in `BACKLOG.md` as RA-nnn under "Re-audit of the merged tree"; the burn-down lanes take them to a
disposition. This file holds the reasoning behind each verdict and the per-slice assessments.

## Confirmed items

| id | severity | category | known | file | title |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| RA-047 | high | correctness | NEW | `modules/AgentFlow/CdpApprover.cs:470` | Sweep breaks on the first prompt READ rather than the first prompt PRESSED, so one standing refused or left-alone prompt on the first-listed webview starves every later webview's approvals and announcements on every tick, and the starved panel appears in no log line |
| RA-145 | high | correctness | NEW | `modules/PetStudio/PetStudioWindow.cs:1217` | An invalid re-parse (a 750 ms typing pause on malformed XML, Open of a rejected file, a refused import) empties the whole behaviour timeline: RenderMap resyncs against the empty node list of a rejected report, and the F165 status then blames the companion |
| RA-011 | medium | regression | KNOWN_FIXED | `docs/agentflow/agentflow_join.py:356` | Paren-less rules (`Edit`, `Write`, `Bash`) match every use of the tool in Claude Code and in the Python harness but are inert in the shipped PermissionRules.cs (and in the JS ruleMatches primitive it ports; the JS compensates in coversPermission), so the README's 21/30 recall row is not what the module would compute (23/30) and the module misjudges calls such rules allow |
| RA-023 | medium | correctness | NEW | `modules/AgentFlow/AgentFlowModule.cs:890` | PressBudget's repeat guard cannot fire on its documented case: a 'clicked' outcome (a dispatched DOM click, not a closed card) clears the identical-press counter on every tick |
| RA-036 | medium | regression | KNOWN_FIXED | `modules/AgentFlow/AgentFlowModule.cs:2733` | The convention runner's own AgentFlow instance (ConventionHost hands null settings, so Mode migrates to Notify) scans the developer's real rules and transcripts and sweeps the live editor on a pool thread while SelfTest runs, contradicting the 'nothing in this run reads real data' comment and racing the process-global receive-buffer count (the open N-scripts-03 flake) |
| RA-046 | medium | security | NEW | `modules/AgentFlow/BlockedDetector.cs:397` | RootExecutable cuts a quoted executable path at its first interior space before taking the leaf, so a path with a space logs a directory-name fragment (`Program`, `Acme`, a first name) into the diagnostic log the module promises carries no path |
| RA-049 | medium | check-cannot-fail | NEW | `modules/AgentFlow/PressBudget.cs:118` | NotePromptCleared on every 'clicked' outcome makes the repeat guard unreachable for its documented case (the click executes, the card stays): a wedged or ignored card is re-clicked and logged every tick with no stand-down, and the guard now fires only for clicks that never executed |
| RA-058 | medium | dangling-call | NEW | `modules/AiBrain/AiBrainModule.cs:868` | SavePaneValues drops TrySetApiKey's false and its message: a key typed with no cloud provider or cloud endpoint selected (or on a DPAPI failure) is silently not stored while Apply reports success |
| RA-063 | medium | regression | KNOWN_FIXED | `modules/AiBrain/engine/AiBrain.cs:500` | SampleDispositionAsync calls the ChooseModel overload that defaults allowSubstitution to true, so a cloud-primary audition ignores SubstituteMissingModel=false and bills five requests to the first catalogue model (R-022 reached ResolveBeforeCapture only) |
| RA-065 | medium | leak | NEW | `modules/AiBrain/engine/AiBrain.cs:995` | AiBrain never records the substitute it sent: UnloadAsync (fullscreen stand-down, retire, shutdown) and the launch warm-up name the configured ids, so under 'keep' a substituted local model is never evicted and outlives the process |
| RA-121 | medium | correctness | NEW | `modules/Fortunes/FortunesModule.cs:864` | 'Select all/none' on the Fortune packs and Genres cards is staged inside the module and survives closing the window: the reopened pane shows the unsaved batch as current beside a pool status that reads the saved state, and the next successful Apply for any field commits it |
| RA-122 | medium | non-operable | NEW | `modules/Fortunes/FortunesModule.cs:873` | After 'Select all/none' with no other pending edit, the rebuilt pane greys Apply while the status says 'Apply to use it': the host re-arms Apply only for field edits it can see, and the ABI gives an action no way to mark the pane dirty |
| RA-151 | medium | correctness | NEW | `modules/Remembrance/CaptureStore.cs:78` | Purge walks every immediate subfolder of storageLocation without testing its name, so a foreign 'recording.wav' (or scratch/snap shape) one level down is deleted after 72 h; the folder dimension is the one un-parsed shape left after 1.0.16 |
| RA-153 | medium | correctness | NEW | `modules/Remembrance/OllamaSummarizer.cs:311` | Ollama model pull has no idle bound on the body stream and no Shutdown cancellation: the untreated twin of the F184 shape in WhisperInstaller.DownloadAsync |
| RA-167 | medium | correctness | NEW | `modules/Remembrance/WhisperInstaller.cs:342` | A Whisper install that fails its run check stays under InstallRoot and is adopted unverified by the next 'Set up' press or the next session's pane Load; the failure result's ExePath/ModelPath are write-only |
| RA-178 | medium | dangling-call | NEW | `modules/Reminder/ReminderModule.cs:1520` | Reminder's five non-pane settings writes ignore Save()'s false (fired set, personal list, briefing stamp, chime pick, migration marker): a failed settings.json write is silent at both layers, "Add a reminder" and "Browse for a chime" report ✓, and a restart with no later successful save re-fires the fired ids still in their lead window, re-fires a once-only personal reminder at the next launch, and loses the typed reminder and the chime pick |

## Refuted (not filed)

- **F020** Output-file assertions rooted at the join directory are real containment checks, not the F023 tautology (root there was derived from the path itself); a traversing leaf is refused today. The four sites exist exactly as quoted (build-installer.ps1:286-290, :359-363, :263 with :449-451; New-RuntimeWixFragment.ps1:179-184), and two more of the same shape exist that the finding did not list (Normalize-MsiDeterminism.ps1:207-212, New-DeterministicPortableZip.ps1:203-209, in the very scripts F023's comment says were repaired). But the central claim, that the containment half is unreachable for any input, is false, and the analogy to F023 does not hold. Assert-DesktopAICompanionOutputF ...
- **F024** The shell-header template does not swallow the glob, grep or search rows: the installed bundles render those headers with no question mark, and BACKLOG.md already records this as refuted. The premise ('most likely ... ending in ?') is false, and the maintainer had already recorded the check. I read the literal from all three installed bundles (anthropic.claude-code-2.1.283, .284 and .285, webview/index.js): each renders `:"Allow this glob command"})`, `:"Allow this grep command"})` and `:"Allow this search"})`, with the closing quote immediately after the last word and no question mark; a grep for `Allow this [a-z]* command[^"]{0,3}` returns exactly the two bare strings. IsShellH ...
- **F127** Shutdown retires _smartGeneration but not _engineGeneration and RebuildEngineAsync never checks _shutdown, yet in the shipped app Shutdown runs only after Application.Run has returned, so the UI-thread continuation that would publish is never dispatched: a contract gap with no reachable effect. The code facts are as stated: Shutdown (FortunesModule.cs:1876-1907) bumps only `_smartGeneration`; `_engineGeneration` is touched only at 230 and 259 (grep), RebuildEngineAsync (257-279) drops a stale result only on the generation compare and never consults `_shutdown` (whose token reaches only the import at 1028 and the download at 1186). But the failure the finding asserts, PublishEngine and a new smart build running after Shutdown, cannot execute in the shipped host, because the continuation ...
- **F149** LastCaptureStopTime includes the keep-alive's WasapiOut stop, but the pinned 3.0.0-preview.6 makes that a millisecond operation (stop event, no latency sleep on the Stop path), so the recorded expectation stands. The finding's premise is that NAudio's WasapiOut.Stop joins a play thread whose loop exit is followed by an unconditional Thread.Sleep(latencyMilliseconds). That is the NAudio 2.x shape, not the pinned NAudio.Wasapi 3.0.0-preview.6 the module ships (Remembrance.csproj PackageReference). Walking the IL of the DLL in the NuGet cache (C:\Users\Admin\.nuget\packages\naudio.wasapi\3.0.0-preview.6\lib\net9.0-windows10.0.19041\NAudio.Wasapi.dll): Stop() sets playbackState=Stopped, calls stopEventWaitHa ...
- **F207** ModuleDeclares reads the loader's module list without a lock, but the list is written only inside StartUp's constructor (LoadFrom) and at ShutdownAll; the Modules pane never loads, updates or unloads a module in the running process, so a pool-thread PlaySound cannot race a pane install or removal and the thread-safety contract holds by immutability, not by a swallowed exception. The code facts in the evidence are accurate (ModuleDeclares enumerates _startUp.LoadedModules, which is ModuleHost.Modules, a lock-free `_loaded.Select(l => l.Module).ToList()` run on the caller's thread; PlaySound wraps everything in `catch { return false; }`), but the claim that this is a race the contract survives only by swallowing is false as stated, because there is no concurrent writer in ordinary use. `_loaded` has exactly two writers in the whole file: `_loaded.Add` at ModuleHost.cs:147 ...
- **F229** Screen-context capture resolves each unique window pid through Process.ProcessName (a full-system NtQuerySystemInformation query) on the UI thread per poke, drop and AI ask; measured at 0.07 to 0.30 ms per pid, about 1 to 5 ms per capture, so no observable stall. The mechanism is described correctly: one Process.GetProcessById(pid).ProcessName per unique pid per Snapshot (DesktopWindows.cs:118 per-call dictionary, :133, :264-284), called from CaptureScreenContext (CompanionHost.cs:385) on the UI thread by FortunesModule.SpeakFortune (:572) and AiBrainModule.Ask (:1345, which says 'we are on the UI thread here' at :1348) and RunAuditionAsync (:520). The cost premise is what fails. The finding reasons that .NET's ProcessName fills the whole system process  ...
- **F327** Nav TextBlock entries stay type-ahead searchable: WPF TextSearch reads FrameworkElement.GetPlainText(), which TextBlock overrides with its text. The F377 facts are as filed: NavItem returns a TextBlock with Text, CharacterEllipsis, ToolTip and AutomationProperties.Name (OptionsWindow.cs:154-159), the nav adds them (:70), the pre-campaign line added strings (commit 2edd62b diff), and TextSearch/IsTextSearchEnabled appear nowhere under src. The conclusion is wrong because the WPF fallback chain the finding relies on omits a step. In dotnet/wpf TextSearch.cs the matcher FindMatchingPrefix creates the primary-text binding only `if (useXml || ...
- **F378** Four-argument SoundBaker constructor is reached only through the one-argument constructor's chain; no external caller remains after the F437/F457 factories. False as stated. The four-argument constructor has a caller: the one-argument public constructor chains into it with the default caps, and that one-argument constructor is the production path from ConvertSkin (Engine.cs:121). Deleting it, as the suggested fix says, would not compile until the one-argument chain is re-pointed at the private six-argument constructor. What is true: no caller outside the class remains. rg for `new SoundBaker(` over the whole repo finds only Engine.cs:121 (one-argume ...
- **F392** The compositor's 1:1 DrawImage paths (Compose, BlitOpaque, Bitmap(Image)) are pixel-exact for every pixel with alpha > 0 on gdiplus; only the RGB under alpha 0 is zeroed, which no consumer can see, so the dedupe comment's 'a 1:1 blit still resamples' does not transfer to these overloads.
